- **import-model**: Use when importing a new model architecture into MAX from a Hugging Face model ID. Triggers on: "import a model into MAX", "add model to MAX", "bring up &lt;HF model&gt; in MAX". When the server runs but output is wrong (gibberish, greedy mismatch, coherent-then-diverges), load debug-model for the divergence hunt instead of scalar-tap iteration.
